The novelty test is a single line — a beam whose endpoint lands\nin a cell the map calls ",["$","em",null,{"children":"confidently free"}]," is a beam the map cannot explain — and it has\ntwo consequences, which are opposite on purpose. Those beams are ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"withheld from\nmapping"}],", so the map never learns the person and does not need to unlearn them. And\ntheir endpoints are ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"injected into the controller's distance field as transient\nobstacles"}],", so MPPI steers around a person who is not in the map at all. Same\nmeasurement, opposite treatment, decided by which downstream consumer is asking."]}]

5c:["$","p",null,{"children":"The interesting engineering question is what happens if the dropout lasts thirty seconds\ninstead of two and a half, and the answer is that the stack behaves correctly and\nuselessly: it sits still, perfectly safe, indefinitely.
